Register access is a direct call `ess_Read32` -&gt; `PSRead32` -&gt; `PSIP_aiehlc::read32` -&gt; XTLM `b_transport`. There is no external entry point, so historically the debug UI refused live reads for `sim_kind == "aiesim"`. - **aigc-reduce-playbook**: 「论文 AIGC 报告复核与表达风险诊断」——把检测报告解析、可疑段落定位、证据补强建议、定向表达修订和复核串成闭环。 适用于中文学位论文/期刊在 PaperYY、PaperPass、知网等平台出现 AIGC 疑似提示，或导师认为文字过度模板化、 证据链不足、论证太顺滑的场景。目标是回到真实材料和学术表达质量，不承诺平台结果、不提供造假方案。 包含平台差异认知、风险定位杠杆、报告解析脚本、 铁律、以及该做/不该做清单。触发词：AIGC报告复核、AIGC疑似、AIGC检测、PaperPass、PaperYY、知网AIGC、 论文AI痕迹、表达太模板化、证据链不足、AI味风险。已整合：理论+替换表(references/theory/)、5维语义检测(references/detector/)、全部脚本(scripts/)，单一入口。 分工（去AI味三角）：语体正向写法→ thesis-style；"像不像AI"的判定→ thesis-deai-review； 本 skill 专管 **平台检测报告**（PaperYY/PaperPass/知网）的解析、定位与合规修订闭环。 ([gxgeek-n/wenqu-mem](https://github.com/gxgeek-n/wenqu-mem/tree/HEAD/skills/aigc-reduce-playbook) · ★3)

Triggered by /ailtir-cowork-plugin:ailtir_bid-planner. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_bid-planner) · ★1)
- **ailtir_case-study-generator**: Converts completed project data into structured case studies, and processes tender debriefs to extract learning signals. Triggered by /ailtir_case-study-generator or debrief mode. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_case-study-generator) · ★1)
- **ailtir_compliance-matrix**: Extracts all ITT requirements into a tracked deliverables matrix. Triggered by /ailtir-cowork-plugin:ailtir_compliance-matrix or when bid-planner runs. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_compliance-matrix) · ★1)
- **ailtir_conductor**: Proactive lifecycle router. Scans every active bid, tells the user what phase each is in, and recommends the next skill to run. Triggered by /ailtir_conductor, or auto-invoked at the end of ailtir_prime when at least one bid exists. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_conductor) · ★1)
- **ailtir_contract-admin**: Drafts contractual notices (Delay, Early Warning, Compensation Event, Loss & Expense, Additional Cost) with correct time bars for the active Ailtir profile. Triggered by /ailtir_contract-admin. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_contract-admin) · ★1)
- **ailtir_contract-risk**: Reviews the tendered contract clause-by-clause against the playbook for the active Ailtir profile (Irish PW-CF/RIAI or UK JCT/NEC4). Triggered by /ailtir-cowork-plugin:ailtir_contract-risk or when bid-planner runs. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_contract-risk) · ★1)
- **ailtir_cost-reconciliation**: Final verification of a construction estimate. Cross-checks against requirements, benchmarks against the active Ailtir profile's cost guides (SCSI for Ireland; BCIS for UK), and identifies gaps. Triggered by /ailtir_cost-reconciliation or step 4 of the estimating workflow. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_cost-reconciliation) · ★1)
- **ailtir_dashboard**: Builds a live interactive HTML dashboard as a Claude Artifact, pulling data from Notion via MCP to visualise the bid pipeline, subcontractor register, and BD KPIs. Triggered by /ailtir_dashboard. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_dashboard) · ★1)
- **ailtir_enable-monitor**: Configures the daily automated opportunity monitor. Connects your email and Notion, and schedules the background task to run every morning. Triggered by /ailtir_enable-monitor. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_enable-monitor) · ★1)
- **ailtir_estimating-workflow**: Master orchestrator for the 4-step construction estimating process, calibrated to the active Ailtir profile (Irish ARM4/NRM2 or UK NRM1/NRM2). Triggered by /ailtir_estimating-workflow or when the user asks to estimate or price a tender. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_estimating-workflow) · ★1)
- **ailtir_feedback**: Captures lightweight anonymous user feedback for an Ailtir workflow: first a 1-10 rating, then the reason, then three structured AskUserQuestion follow-ups derived from the current session. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_feedback) · ★1)
- **ailtir_go-no-go**: Evaluates bid viability against the accreditation gates and weighted scoring matrix appropriate to the active Ailtir profile (Irish CIRI/Safe-T-Cert or UK SSIP). Triggered by /ailtir_go-no-go or when bid-planner runs. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_go-no-go) · ★1)
- **ailtir_ingest**: Routes messy data dropped into an Ailtir workspace (subcontractor quotes, tender addendums, RFIs, emails) into the right project folder and updates Notion databases. Always confirms before writing. Triggered by /ailtir_ingest or when the user drops files into the workspace without clear instructions. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_ingest) · ★1)
- **ailtir_intelligence-builder**: Builds and maintains the Ailtir Intelligence knowledge base through interviews or bulk ingestion of historical tender material. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_intelligence-builder) · ★1)
- **ailtir_notion-second-brain**: An advanced setup skill that builds a complete construction company "second brain" in Notion, including SOPs, cost history, and lessons learned. Triggered by /ailtir_notion-second-brain. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_notion-second-brain) · ★1)
- **ailtir_notion-setup**: Builds the required Notion databases for an Ailtir workspace via the Notion MCP connector. Creates the Bid Pipeline, Subcontractor Directory, RFI Log, and CRM databases with the correct properties and relations. USE THIS when the user runs /ailtir_notion-setup or asks to set up their Notion databases. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_notion-setup) · ★1)
- **ailtir_opportunity-monitor**: Automated daily background task that checks your email for tender alerts appropriate to your active Ailtir profile (Irish eTenders/TED or UK Find a Tender/Contracts Finder), scores each opportunity against your company profile using a 5-dimension strategic fit model, and logs qualified leads to your Notion Bid Pipeline. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_opportunity-monitor) · ★1)
- **ailtir_package-breakdown**: Phase 2 skill. Converts project documents (head contract, specs, drawings) into a subcontractor trade package register and scope matrix. Triggered by /ailtir_package-breakdown. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_package-breakdown) · ★1)
- **ailtir_post-tender-interview**: Prepares the contractor team for a post-tender interview (CWMF interview under `ireland-gc`; UK public/private post-submission clarification interview under `uk-gc`). Generates presentation outlines, Q&A prep, and key talking points. Triggered by /ailtir_post-tender-interview or when the user asks to prep for a tender interview. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_post-tender-interview) · ★1)
- **ailtir_pqq-manager**: Auto-fills PQQs/SQ documents from the company context, or evaluates incoming subcontractor PQQs, applying the accreditation and disclosure requirements of the active Ailtir profile (Irish CIRI/Safe-T-Cert or UK SSIP/Modern Slavery/Carbon Reduction Plan). Triggered by /ailtir_pqq-manager. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_pqq-manager) · ★1)
- **ailtir_prelims-builder**: Builds a priced Schedule of Preliminaries using the measurement structure of the active Ailtir profile (Irish ARM4 for PW-CF, UK NRM1 for JCT/NEC4). Triggered by /ailtir_prelims-builder or when the user asks to price prelims. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_prelims-builder) · ★1)
- **ailtir_prime**: Session initialization. Syncs Notion databases to local markdown cache and presents a briefing. Triggered by /ailtir_prime. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_prime) · ★1)
- **ailtir_programme-builder**: Generates a tender programme (Gantt schedule) and narrative. Adapts phrasing to the active Ailtir profile — CWMF requirements for Ireland, Procurement Act 2023 / JCT / NEC4 requirements for UK. Triggered by /ailtir_programme-builder or when the user asks for a project schedule. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_programme-builder) · ★1)
- **ailtir_project-indexer**: Index a construction project folder into three reusable markdown context files (CLAUDE.md, project.md, and drawings.md) so future sessions can reason about the project from cheap text instead of re-parsing PDFs every time. Triggered by /ailtir_project-indexer, or during bid-planner execution. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_project-indexer) · ★1)
- **ailtir_quality-writer**: Drafts technical method statements, social value (CBC) responses, and self-scores drafts against ITT evaluation descriptors. Triggered by /ailtir_quality-writer. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_quality-writer) · ★1)
- **ailtir_rate-library**: Provides current construction cost rates (labour, materials, m² benchmarks) for the active Ailtir profile (Irish SEO/SCSI or UK CIJC/BCIS). Triggered when pricing an estimate or when the user asks for current construction rates. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_rate-library) · ★1)
- **ailtir_rfi-generator**: Drafts formal Requests for Information (RFIs), logs them in Notion, and processes incoming RFI answers to flag impacts on other bid documents. Triggered by /ailtir_rfi-generator or when the user says "draft an RFI" or "process these RFI answers". ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_rfi-generator) · ★1)
- **ailtir_setup**: First-time Ailtir workspace setup. Runs the onboarding interview and builds your local Context folder. Triggered by /ailtir_setup. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_setup) · ★1)
- **ailtir_site-diary**: Transforms rough field notes or voice transcripts into a formal daily site diary. Under `ireland-gc` the diary is compliant with PW-CF requirements; under `uk-gc` it captures the equivalent daily record required to support NEC4 Compensation Event assessment and JCT Extension of Time / Loss & Expense claims. Triggered by /ailtir_site-diary or when the user drops field notes. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_site-diary) · ★1)
- **ailtir_subcontractor-enquiry**: Prepares subcontractor enquiry packs based on the package breakdown. Triggered by /ailtir_subcontractor-enquiry. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_subcontractor-enquiry) · ★1)
- **ailtir_submission-preflight**: Runs final deterministic compliance checks before submission, targeting the portal appropriate to the active Ailtir profile (Irish eTenders or UK Find a Tender / Contracts Finder). Triggered by /ailtir_submission-preflight. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_submission-preflight) · ★1)
- **ailtir_takeoff**: Extracts elemental quantities from construction drawings (PDF) into an NRM2 elemental Excel register. NRM2 is common across `ireland-gc` and `uk-gc` profiles. Triggered by /ailtir_takeoff or when the user asks to measure drawings. ([Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin](https://github.com/Team-Ailtir/ailtir-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ailtir_takeoff) · ★1)
- **aim**: When data lands with a thin ask — files, a repo, a paste, a chat scrollback — read it and propose what the user is trying to do with it, as a proposal to confirm rather than a question to answer. Use the moment material arrives without a fully-formed ask, or when the ask reads thinner than the data suggests. ([LilMGenius/paperthin](https://github.com/LilMGenius/paperthin/tree/HEAD/skills/depth/aim) · ★1.1k · 1k installs · 3 copies)
- **aim**: Clarify the outcome you want - a change in user behavior, not a feature shipped. Use at the start of any work to ground the session in strategic intent. ([open-horizon-labs/repo-native-alignment](https://github.com/open-horizon-labs/repo-native-alignment/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/aim) · ★5)
- **aim-and-hypothesis-designer**: Designs primary aims, secondary aims, and testable hypotheses from broad biomedical research ideas. Use this skill when a user needs to convert a loose study idea into a tighter protocol-framing structure with clear aim hierarchy, hypothesis discipline, and separation between hypothesis-driven and exploratory components. Always keep aims answerable, non-overlapping, and aligned to the intended evidence type and study scope. ([aipoch/medical-research-skills](https://github.com/aipoch/medical-research-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome-med-research-skills/Protocol%20Design/aim-and-hypothesis-designer) · ★1.9k)
- **aim-before-build**: Compare a desired outcome with the real codebase before implementation and classify it as already done, partial, not started, or conflicting. Use for “check before building”, “is this already implemented?”, “조준”, or requests that combine a goal with status discovery. ([Jason-hub-star/model-orchestrator](https://github.com/Jason-hub-star/model-orchestrator/tree/HEAD/skills/aim-before-build))

- **aimeat-app-builder**: Build and publish AIMEAT apps. This OpenHands is wired to an AIMEAT node over MCP (the aimeat_* tools). When the user asks to build, make, or publish an app, a game, a tool, or anything "on AIMEAT" / "to aimeat.io", follow the workflow below. ([miikkij/aimeat-protocol](https://github.com/miikkij/aimeat-protocol/tree/HEAD/tools/aimeat-openhands/skills/aimeat-app-builder) · ★8)
- **aimeat-app-building**: The research-first precondition for building any app on AIMEAT, and the repo-side rules for the app-building prompt system and the served browser SDK libs. Use when the task is to build, make, publish or fork an AIMEAT application (game or tool), or when editing src/services/build-app-prompt.ts, /v1/app-templates, or anything under src/static/sdk-libs/. ([miikkij/aimeat-protocol](https://github.com/miikkij/aimeat-protocol/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/aimeat-app-building) · ★8)
- **aimeat-design-language**: The AIMEAT design language in words and in numbers: the two faces (showroom outside, poster inside), the three type tokens every font on the site descends from, the four shapes, the colours, the wordmark, and the one place a value is changed (theme.css tokens) with the map of every surface a token reaches. Use before designing or styling anything that carries the AIMEAT name, before changing a font or a colour, and to judge whether a screen looks like this product. ([miikkij/aimeat-protocol](https://github.com/miikkij/aimeat-protocol/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/aimeat-design-language) · ★8)
- **aimeat-frontend-verify**: AIMEAT frontend rules: the styling conventions to write to, and how a finished change is verified by driving a real browser through the Playwright MCP server (three viewports, repaint count, idle network log). Use before writing or editing anything under public/views, public/components, public/js, public/css, locales or an HTML page, and again when that change is done or an AIMEAT app has been published. ([miikkij/aimeat-protocol](https://github.com/miikkij/aimeat-protocol/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/aimeat-frontend-verify) · ★8)
- **aimeat-imagery**: How images for AIMEAT are produced with scripts/gen_image.py instead of stock or placeholder art, including the upload log to check before regenerating. Use whenever a task needs an app icon, banner, hero graphic, illustration, logo, og-image or any other picture.
